Reconstructed vocabulary and proposed Indus readings

All 178 existing pair recipes were searched against the full 15,629-record table. Different sources and ancestral levels remain separate. Exact reconstruction equality supports a lexical possibility; it does not establish a sign meaning or date the word to the Indus period. Missing coverage and unequal forms remain unresolved.
